or the “Company”), I am pleased to present our annual report of the         「本公司」）董事會，欣然呈報本公司及其附屬
Company and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) for the year ended December      公司（「本集團」）截至2022年12月31日止年度
31, 2022.                                                                   之年報。

In the past 28 years, Risun has a very clear vision, the Group is always    回首過往28載崢嶸芳華，旭陽秉承極其清晰
achieving to be the World’s leading energy chemicals company – future       明確的願景。立足於「創新引領未來」的實現
rooted in innovation. As the occasion of setting up the headquarters        方式，本集團始終矢志成為「世界領先的能
in Beijing in early 20th century, Risun moved into Risun Plaza, the new     源化工公司」。恰逢北京總部設立二十週年

     15,874,000 shares at an average price of HK$3.53 representing 0.36% to       銷股份15,874,000股，佔截至2022年1月1日
     the issued share of 4,440,000,000 shares as at January 1, 2022.              已發行股份4,440,000,000股的0.36%。

     Effective from March 7, 2022, the Company was switched from Hang             本公司由恒生綜合指數（小型股）轉為恒生綜
     Seng Composite Index (SmallCap stocks) to Hang Seng Composite Index          合指數（中型股）及由恒生小型股（可投資）指
     (MidCap stocks) and switched from Hang Seng Small Cap (Investable)           數轉為恒生大中型股（可投資）指數，自2022
     Index to Hang Seng Large-Mid Cap (investable) Index. The change of           年3月7日起生效。本公司獲變更市值分類，
     market capitalization groupings reflected the market’s recognition of the    反映市場對本公司業務表現和前景的認同。
     Company’s business performance and prospects.

PROSPECTS                                                                     展望

Although the Group was in a cyclical industry in China and overseas, the      儘管本集團在中國及海外市場屬於週期性行
business model and regional layout in China would be less cyclical by:        業，但在中國的商業模式及區域佈局將通過
                                                                              以下方式降低週期性：

1.    Leveling both coke and refined chemicals products, at which they        1.   平衡好具有不同需求週期的焦炭及精細
      had different demand cycles. Some refined chemicals with strong              化工產品。部分需求旺盛的精細化工產
      demand side allowed us to maintain the business growth;                      品使我們得以保持業務增長；

2.    Making use of the leading position and experience in coke               2.    憑藉於焦炭生產方面的領先地位及經
      production, the Group could expand the business by achieving                  驗，本集團通過併購與運營管理服務達
      the balance of merger and acquisition together with operation                 致平衡來拓展業務。這即是「輕資產」與
      management services. This is the balance of “asset-light” and                「重資產」投資策略的平衡；
      “capital intensive” investment strategies;

3.    Developing new materials and new energy on top of coke and              3.   新材料、新能源開發與焦炭、精細化工
      refined chemicals. This could balance the traditional coke and               產品並駕齊驅。這可以平衡傳統焦炭與
      refined chemicals business and the purified hydrogen business;               精細化工業務以及純氫業務；及
      and

4.    Enhancing the Group’s competitive strengths through automation          4.   通過自動化及信息化技術增強本集團的
      and information technologies.                                                競爭優勢。

The Group aimed to level the periodic up and down and rooted on our           本集團的目標是平衡週期性的起伏波動並植
vision and strategies. The Group determined to take into account of           根於我們的願景與戰略。本集團決心順應中
national macro economy of China to lead the business development of the       國的宏觀經濟趨勢，引領本集團業務發展。
Group. It is believed that China’s economy will continue to be prosperous     我們堅信中國經濟將繼續繁榮昌盛，本集團
and it is the Group’s initiative to assume high standard of environmental     亦主動承擔高標準的環境及社會責任，追求
and social responsibilities and pursue the coordination between national      國家興企業之間的協同發展。
development and its own business development.

Moreover, the Group believed the success would be resulted from the           此外，本集團深信成功將源自供應商、客
recognition and support from our suppliers, customers, shareholders           戶、股東及全體員工的認可和鼎力支持。旭
and all employees. Risun would continue to uphold the philosophy of           陽將繼續秉承「以客戶為中心，為客戶創造價
“customer-centric and creating value for customers”, achieve win-win          值」的經營理念，實現業務共贏發展，推動焦
business development and promote the innovation and development in            炭、新材料及新能源行業的創新與發展。
the coke, new materials and new energy industries.

Looking forward in 2023, the Group will continue to increase the market       展望2023年，本集團將繼續透過拓展運營管
share of all key products by expanding the operation management               理以及併購增加旗艦產品的市場份額。本集
together with merger and acquisition. The Group also plans to strengthen      團亦計劃強化焦炭及精細化工產品生產線，
the production line of coke and refined chemicals and further refining into   深入推進新材料、新能源的精細化，例如己
new materials and new energy such as caprolactam (CPL) and hydrogen-          內醯胺(CPL)及氫基產品。本集團根據當前國
based products. The main theme is to enlarge the production of the            內外經濟形勢，奏響擴大現有產品組合生產
existing products mix and manufacture some new refined chemicals              並製造新型精細化工產品的主旋律。
products according to the current economic situation in China and
overseas.

DIVIDEND                                                                     股息

In view of balancing the satisfactory operating results and business         為平衡令人滿意的經營業績以及未來業務前
prospects in future, the Board recommends a final dividend amounting         景，董事會建議2022年末期股息為人民幣
to RMB39.8 million for 2022 and the total dividend amount for the full       39.8百萬元，2022年全年股息總額為人民幣
year 2022 of RMB584.1 million, representing no less than 30% to the net      584.1百萬元，不低於本集團年度可分配淨利
annual distributable profits of the Group of approximately RMB1.9 billion,   潤約人民幣19億元的30%，作為對本公司股
as a return to the shareholders of the Company.                              東的回報。

to our suppliers, customers and banks for their continuous support. The      商、客戶及往來銀行）的持續支持。董事會及
Board and all management are looking forward to achieve another year         全體管理層成員期待在2023年乘勢而上，奮
with momentum, growth and expansion in 2023.                                 楫篤行，實現穩增長、促發展。

The Group is an integrated coke, coking chemical and refined chemical          本集團為中國的焦炭、焦化產品和精細化工
producer and supplier together with relevant operation management              產品綜合生產商及供應商及相關運營管理服

volume in 2022, according to Frost & Sullivan (Beijing) Inc., Shanghai         若斯特沙利文」），按2022年生產╱加工量計
Branch Co., (“Frost & Sullivan”) an independent global consulting firm.        算，本集團仍然是全球最大的獨立焦炭生產
                                                                               商及供應商。

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                 業務回顧

The Group’s vertically integrated business model and its experience of          本集團縱向一體化的業務模式及在焦炭化工
more than 27 years in the coke industry production chain enables the            業生產鏈逾27年的經驗使本集團能夠拓展下
Group to widen the downstream refined chemicals industry. Currently,            游精細化工產品市場。目前，本集團有四個
the Group has four business segments ranging from coke and coking               業務分部，包括焦炭及焦化產品生產、精細
chemicals manufacturing, refined chemicals (including hydrogen                  化工產品（包括氫氣產品）生產、運營管理服
products) manufacturing, operation management services and trading.             務及貿易。於報告期內及直至本報告日期，
During the Reporting Period and up to the date of this report, the Group        本集團新訂了四份運營管理協議，內容有
entered into four new operation management agreements in relation to            關中國山東省的三個項目及中國山西省的一
three projects in Shandong Province, China and one project in Shanxi            個項目，進一步擴大本集團於焦炭及焦化產
Province, China to further expand the Group’s business presence in coke         品、煤焦油以及苯加氫領域的業務佈局。
and coking chemicals, coal tar and hydrogenated benzene sectors.

Apart from the exiting four business segments, the Group actively               除現有的四個業務分部外，本集團亦擬積極
participated into the hydrogen industrialization plan in Hebei Dingzhou,        參與位於中國河北定州、內蒙古呼和浩特及
Inner Mongolia Hohhot and Hebei Xingtai, China. The Group aimed at              河北邢台的氫氣工業化計劃。本集團矢志成
creating a clean and low-carbon hydrogen energy supplier. Focusing              為清潔及低碳氫氣能源供應商。本集團聚焦
on the rapid development of hydrogen energy industry in Beijing-Tianjin-        於京津冀地區的氫氣能源行業的高速發展，
Hebei area, the Group is committed to develop from production, storage,         致力同時發展生產、儲存、運輸、加氫以至

      Coke                                                                           焦炭

      The coke industry was under the supply-demand reform in the previous           焦炭行業於過去數年進行供需改革，並將於
      years, which will continue in 2023. The reform is to eliminate the coke        2023年繼續進行。改革旨在消除高污染焦炭
      production capacity from high-polluting coke enterprises. Coking               企業的焦炭產能。過往三年，中國4.3米及以
      furnaces of 4.3 meters or below in China had ceased operation in the past      下焦爐已停止運行，且未來將繼續於中國不
      3 years and will continue to shut down in different provinces of China,        同省份關停，焦炭需求將由其他焦炭企業現
      and the demand of coke will be supplemented by the other existing coke         有的其他焦炭產能補充。本集團認為焦炭的
      production capacity from other coke enterprises. The Group believes that       售價將獲益於供求關係改革。
      the selling price of coke will benefit from the supply-demand reform.

      The Group expands the coke production/processing capacity by different         本集團以不同方式擴大焦炭生產╱加工能
      ways, such as merger and acquisition of existing coke enterprises, setting     力，例如併購現有焦炭企業、與其他焦炭╱

      that our market share of the industry of independent coke producer and         幾年，我們於中國及亞太區的獨立焦炭生產
      supplier will increase from currently 1.6% to a high single-digit percentage   商及供應商行業市場份額將由目前的1.6%

EXECUTIVE DIRECTORS                                                        執行董事

Mr. YANG Xuegang (楊雪崗), aged 58, is an Executive Director, the chief       楊雪崗先生，58歲，為本公司執行董事、行
executive officer of the Company and the chairman of the Board. He was     政總裁兼董事會主席。彼於2007年11月獲委
appointed as an Executive Director in November 2007. He is responsible     任為執行董事。彼負責本集團的整體管理及
for the overall management and business development of the Group. He is    業務發展。彼亦為提名委員會主席。
also the chairman of the Nomination Committee.

Mr. Yang graduated from Hebei Hydraulic College ( 河 北 水 利 專 科 學 校 )        楊先生於1985年7月畢業於中國滄州河北水
(now known as Hebei University of Water Resources and Electric             利專科學校（現稱河北水利電力學院），獲授

Relationship with Employees                                                      與僱員的關係

The Group recruits new employees based on specific job requirements,             本集團按不時的特定工作要求、資源及需求
our resources and needs from time to time. The Group provides technical          招聘新僱員。本集團向僱員提供技術及運營
as well as operational training for our employees and the Group has              培訓，且本集團已為我們的管理人員定制特

DIVIDENDS AND DIVIDEND POLICY                                                股息及股息政策

After obtaining the shareholders’ approval at the 2021 annual general        於2022年5月27日在2021年度股東週年大會
meeting on May 27, 2022, the Company paid a final dividend of RMB6.3         上獲股東批准後，本公司以現金向於2022年
cents per share (tax inclusive) for the year ended December 31, 2021 in      6月20日名列本公司股份登記冊的股東派發截
cash to shareholders whose names appeared on the register of members         至2021年12月31日止年度的末期股息每股人

SHARE OPTION SCHEME                                                                      購股權計劃

the Share Option Scheme (the “Share Option Scheme”). The terms of the                    購股權計劃（「購股權計劃」）。購股權計劃的
Share Option Scheme are in compliance with the provisions of Chapter 17                  條款符合上市規則第17章的規定。
of the Listing Rules.




                                                                                                                       2022 年報
                                                                                                                  中國旭陽集團有限公司
                                                                                                                                    61
      Report of the Board of Directors (Continued)
      董事會報告（續）


      PURPOSE                                                                      目的

      The purpose of the Share Option Scheme is to provide incentives to           購股權計劃旨在向參與者（定義見下段）提供
      Participants (as defined in the following paragraph) to contribute to the    鼓勵以向本公司作出貢獻並激勵彼等作出更
      Company and motivate them to higher levels of performance, and to            佳表現，以及使本公司能夠招聘高質素僱員
      enable the Company to recruit high caliber employees and attract or          並吸引或挽留對本集團具價值的人力資源。
      retain human resources that are valuable to the Group.

      WHO MAY JOIN                                                                 可參與人士

      The Board may, at its absolute discretion, offer options to subscribe for     董事會可全權酌情根據購股權計劃所載的條
      such number of Shares in accordance with the terms set out in the Share       款向本集團任何成員公司的任何僱員、執行
      Option Scheme (“Options”) to any employee, executive director or non-         董事或非執行董事（包括獨立非執行董事）
      executive director (including independent non-executive director) of any     （「參與者」）提呈可認購有關數目的股份的購

When it applied for the listing of its Shares, the Company applied to the       於申請股份上市時，本公司已向聯交所申

waiver that the minimum public float requirement under Rule 8.08(1) of          規則第8.08(1)條項下之最低公眾持股量規定
the Hong Kong Listing Rules be reduced and the minimum percentage               的豁免，本公司之最低公眾持股量可予降低
of the Shares from time to time held by the public to be the higher of (i)      惟公眾不時持有的股份最低百分比須為以下

NOMINATION OF DIRECTORS AND BOARD                                              董事提名及董事會多元化政策
DIVERSITY POLICY

In order to enhance the quality of the performance of the Board and            為了提高董事會的業績質量並支持實現本公
to support the attainment of the Company’s strategic objectives and            司的策略性目標及可持續發展，我們已採納
sustainable development, we have adopted a board diversity policy              董事會多元化政策（「董事會多元化政策」）。
(the “Board Diversity Policy”). Pursuant to the Board Diversity Policy, we     根據董事會多元化政策，我們在選擇董事會
seek to achieve Board diversity through the consideration of a number          人選時考慮多個因素，包括但不限於性別、
of factors when selecting candidates to the Board, including but not           技能、年齡、專業經驗、知識、文化及教育
limited to gender, skills, age, professional experience, knowledge,            背景、種族及服務年期，以求實現董事會多
cultural and education background, ethnicity and length of service.            元化。最終將按選定候選人的長處及將為董
Appointments will ultimately be based on merit and the contributions the       事會所作貢獻而作委任。董事局相信以唯才
selected candidates will bring to the Board. The Board believes that such      是用之原則委任董事將最有利於本公司繼續
merit-based appointments will best enable the Company to service our           為其股東以至其他持份者服務。
Shareholders and other stakeholders.

The Nomination Committee is responsible for reviewing the structure,           提名委員會負責審閱董事會架構、成員人數
number of members, and composition of the Board of Directors. In               及組成。此外，委員會根據本公司的策略計
addition, the Committee makes recommendations to the Board relating to         劃、業務營運、資產規模及股權架構向董事
the size and structure of the Board of Directors based on the Company’s        會就有關董事會規模及架構提供建議。委員
strategic plans, business operations, asset size and shareholding              會亦會討論及審查相關董事的甄選標準、提
structure. The Committee also discusses and reviews the selection              名及委任程序，並向董事會提供建議。在甄
standard, the nomination and appointment process of relevant Directors,        選候選人時，本公司根據組織章程細則通過
and makes recommendations to the Board of Directors. In identifying            招聘機構、現有董事轉介及本公司股東推薦
candidates, the Company solicits candidates through recruitment                等方式招募候選人。候選人將由董事會批准
agencies, referrals by existing directors, and recommendation by               通過。
shareholders of the Company in accordance with the Articles of
Association. The candidates will be approved by the Board of Directors.

The Board Diversity Policy needs to be followed when the Nomination            在提名委員會就候選人提供建議或當董事會
Committee makes recommendations on the candidates or when the                  作出提名時需要遵循董事會多元化政策。提
Board of Directors makes nomination. The Nomination Committee is               名委員會負責監督董事會多元化政策的實施

4.   BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED                                      4.   綜合財務報表的編製基準及主
     FINANCIAL STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT                                           要會計政策
     ACCOUNTING POLICIES

     The consolidated financial statements has been prepared in                     綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理事
     accordance with IFRSs issued by the IASB. For the purpose of                   會頒佈的國際財務報告準則編製。就編
     preparation of the consolidated financial statements, information              製綜合財務報表之目的而言，當可合理
     is considered material if such information is reasonably expected              預期該等資料會影響主要使用者之決
     to influence decisions made by primary users. In addition, the                 策，該等資料則被視為屬重大。此外，
     consolidated financial statements includes applicable disclosures              綜合財務報表包括聯交所證券上市規則
     required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the               及香港公司條例規定的適用披露。
     Stock Exchange and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

     The consolidated financial statements has been prepared on the                 綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編製，
     historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments that            惟若干金融工具如下文載列的會計政策
     are measured at fair values at the end of each reporting period, as            所述於各報告期末以公允值計量除外。
     explained in the accounting policies below.

     Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the                    歷史成本通常基於換取商品及服務交易
     consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                        所付出代價的公允值。

     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or             公允值是於計量日期由市場參與者於有
     paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market          秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債
     participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that               須支付的價格，而不論該價格是否直接
     price is directly observable or estimated using another valuation              可觀察或可使用其他估值技術估計。若
     technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability,            市場參與者於計量日期對資產或負債定
     the Group takes into account the characteristics of the asset or               價時會考慮資產或負債的特點，則本集
     liability if market participants would take those characteristics into         團於估計資產或負債的公允值時會考慮
     account when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement                 該等特點。綜合財務報表中作計量及╱
     date. Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in                 或披露用途的公允值乃按此基準釐定，
     the consolidated financial statements is determined on such a                  惟根據國際財務報告準則第16號 「租
     basis, except for leasing transactions that are accounted for in               賃」入賬的租賃交易，以及與公允值有
     accordance with IFRS 16 Leases, and measurements that have                     部分相若地方但並非公允值的計量，譬
     some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as net            如國際會計準則第2號「存貨」內的可變
     realizable value in IAS 2 Inventories or value in use in IAS 36                現淨值或國際會計準則第36號「資產減
     Impairment of Assets.                                                          值」的使用價值除外。

     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account           非金融資產的公允值計量須計及一名市
     a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by                場參與者透過使用其資產的最高及最佳
     using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another        用途或透過將資產出售予將使用其最高
     market participant that would use the asset in its highest and best            及最佳用途的另一名市場參與者而能夠
     use.                                                                           產生經濟利益的能力。

     For financial instruments which are transferred at fair value and              按公允值轉讓的金融工具，凡於其後期
     a valuation technique that unobservable inputs is to be used to                間應用以不可觀察輸入數據計量公允值
     measure fair value in subsequent periods, the valuation technique is           的估值方法，估值方法應予校正，以使
     calibrated so that the results of the valuation technique equals the           估值方法的結果相等於交易價格。
     transaction price.

       綜合財務報表附註（續）

       4.     BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED                                      4.   綜合財務報表的編製基準及主
              FINANCIAL STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT                                           要會計政策（續）
              ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)

              In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value                      此外，就財務申報而言，公允值計量根
              measurements are categorized into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the                 據公允值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度及
              degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are                  輸入數據對公允值計量整體的重要性分
              observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value                類為第一級、第二級或第三級，其載述
              measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:                   如下：

              •       Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active                •   第一級輸入數據是實體於計量日
                      markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can            期可以取得的相同資產或負債於
                      access at the measurement date;                                            活躍市場的報價（未經調整）；

              •       Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices included           •    第二級輸入數據是就資產或負債
                      within Level 1, that are observable for the asset or liability,             直接或間接地可觀察的輸入數據
                      either directly or indirectly; and                                         （第一級內包括的報價除外）；及

              •       Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or                •   第三級輸入數據是資產或負債的
                      liability.                                                                 不可觀察輸入數據。

              The principal accounting policies adopted are set out below.                   所採納的主要會計政策載於下文。

              Basis of consolidation                                                         綜合基準

              The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                綜合財務報表包含本公司及本公司控制
              statements of the Company and entities controlled by the Company               的實體及其附屬公司的財務報表。本公
              and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved where the Company:                   司於以下情況擁有控制權：

              •       has power over the investee;                                           •   可對投資對象行使權力；

              •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                •   因參與投資對象之營運而承受所
                      involvement with the investee; and                                         得浮動回報之風險或享有回報之
                                                                                                 權利；及

              •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                •   能行使權力影響回報。

              The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts           倘有事實及情況顯示上述三個控制因素
              and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more               其中一個或以上有變，本集團會重新評
              of the three elements of control listed above.                                 估其是否控制投資對象。

              Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains                    本集團在取得附屬公司的控制權時開始
              control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses                    將該附屬公司綜合入賬，並於失去該附
              control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses                   屬公司的控制權時終止綜合入賬。具體
              of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are                    而言，於年內所收購或出售的附屬公司
              included in the consolidated statements of profit or loss and other            的收入及開支由本集團取得控制權當日
              comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control until               開始計入綜合損益及其他全面收益表，
              the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                      直至本集團不再控制該附屬公司當日為
                                                                                             止。

     Business combinations                                                          業務合併

     A business is an integrated set of activities and assets which                 業務指一組綜合活動及資產，包括投入
     includes an input and a substantive process that together                      及實質過程，且兩者對創造產出的能力
     significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs. The acquired        有重大貢獻。倘收購過程對繼續生產產
     processes are considered substantive if they are critical to the               出的能力至關重要，則該收購流程被視
     ability to continue producing outputs, including an organised                  為具實質性，包括具備執行相關過程所
     workforce with the necessary skills, knowledge, or experience to               必需的技能、知識或經驗的組織勞動
     perform the related processes or they significantly contribute to the          力，或對持續生產產出的能力有重大貢
     ability to continue producing outputs and are considered unique                獻，且均被認為屬獨特或稀缺，或彼等
     or scarce or cannot be replaced without significant cost, effort, or           在無重大成本、努力或持續生產產出能
     delay in the ability to continue producing outputs.                            力出現延遲的情況下不可取代。

     Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the acquisition             收購業務以收購法入賬。於業務合併中
     method. The consideration transferred in a business combination                所轉讓之代價按公允值計量，即本集團
     is measured at fair value, which is calculated as the sum of the               為換取被收購方控制權而轉收的資產、
     acquisition-date fair values of the assets transferred by the Group,           發生或承擔的負債及發行的權益工具於
     liabilities incurred by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree         收購日期的公允值總和計算。收購相關
     and the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for control           成本一般於產生時於損益內確認。
     of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally recognised
     in profit or loss as incurred.

     For business combinations in which the acquisition date is on or               就收購日期於2022年1月1日或之後的
     after January 1, 2022, the identifiable assets acquired and liabilities        業務合併而言，所收購的可識別資產及
     assumed must meet the definitions of an asset and a liability in the           所承擔的負債必須符合國際會計準則理
     Conceptual Framework for Financial Reporting issued by IASB in                 事會於2018年3月發佈的2018年財務
     March 2018 (the “Conceptual Framework”) except for transactions                報告概念框架（「概念框架」）內資產及負
     and events within the scope of IAS 37 Provisions, Contingent                   債的定義，惟國際會計準則第37號  「撥
     Liabilities and Contingent Assets or IFRIC 21 Levies, in which                 備、或然負債及或然資產」或國際財務
     the Group applies IAS 37 or IFRIC 21 instead of the Conceptual                 報告詮釋委員會詮釋第21號 「徵費」範
     Framework to identify the liabilities it has assumed in a business             圍內的交易及事件除外，於該情況下，
     combination. Contingent assets are not recognised.                             本集團應用國際會計準則第37號或國
                                                                                    際財務報告詮釋委員會詮釋第21號而
                                                                                    非概念框架以識別其於業務合併中所承

              any.

              For the purposes of impairment testing, goodwill is allocated to             就減值測試而言，商譽會被分配至預期
              each of the Group’s cash-generating units (or group of cash-                 因合併的協同效應而獲益的各有關現金
              generating units) that is expected to benefit from the synergies             產生單位（或現金產生單位組別），即指
              of the combination, which represent the lowest level at which the            就內部管理而言監控商譽的最低層級且
              goodwill is monitored for internal management purposes and not               不大於經營分部的單位。
              larger than an operating segment.

              A cash-generating unit (or group of cash-generating units) to which          獲分配商譽的現金產生單位（或現金產
              goodwill has been allocated is tested for impairment annually or             生單位組別）會每年進行減值測試，或
              more frequently when there is indication that the unit (or a group of        於有跡象顯示有關單位或（現金產生單
              cash-generating units) may be impaired. For goodwill arising on an           位組別）可能出現減值時更頻密地進行
              acquisition in a reporting period, the cash-generating unit (or group        減值測試。就於報告期內因收購而產生
              of cash-generating units) to which goodwill has been allocated               的商譽而言，獲分配商譽的現金產生單
              is tested for impairment before the end of that reporting period.            位（或現金產生單位組別）於該報告期末
              If the recoverable amount is less than its carrying amount, the              前進行減值測試。若可收回金額少於其
              impairment loss is allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of          賬面值，則減值損失會先分配以扣減任
              any goodwill and then to the other assets on a pro-rata basis based          何商譽的賬面值，其後按該單位（或現
              on the carrying amount of each asset in the unit (or group of cash-          金產生單位組別）內各項資產賬面值的
              generating units).                                                           比例分配至其他資產。

     of control of an arrangement, which exists only when decisions                約協定共享對安排的控制權，僅於與相
     about the relevant activities require unanimous consent of the                關活動有關的決策須獲共享控制權的訂
     parties sharing control.                                                      約方一致同意時存在。

              Revenue from contracts with customers                                           客戶合約收益

              The Group recognizes revenue when (or as) a performance                         本集團於完成履約責任時（或就此）確認
              obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the products or services        收益，即於特定履約責任相關的產品或
              underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred to              服務的「控制權」轉讓予客戶時。
              the customers.

              A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a bundle              履約責任指一個明確貨品或服務（或一
              of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct goods or         批明確貨品或服務）或一系列大致相同
              services that are substantially the same.                                       的明確貨品或服務。

              Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognized over                 倘符合以下其中一項條件，控制權會按
              time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of              時間轉移，而收益則參考完全履行相關
              the relevant performance obligation if one of the following criteria is         履約責任的進度按時間確認：
              met:

              •       the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the                   •   於本集團履約時，客戶同時取得
                      benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the Group                   並耗用實體履約所提供的利益；
                      performs;

              •       the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that               •   本集團的履約創造及增強一項資
                      the customer controls as the Group performs; or                             產，而該項資產於本集團履約時
                                                                                                  由客戶控制；或

              •       the Group’s performance does not create an asset with                   •   本集團的履約並未創造讓本集團
                      an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                        有替代用途的資產，且本集團對
                      enforceable right to payment for performance completed to                   迄今已完成履約的付款具有可強
                      date.                                                                       制執行的權利。

              Otherwise, revenue is recognized at a point in time when the                    否則，收益於客戶獲得商品或服務的控
              customer obtains control of the goods or services.                              制權的時點確認。

              A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer              合約負債指本集團向客戶轉移本集團已
              goods or services to a customer for which the Group has received                向客戶收取代價（或應收取代價金額）的
              consideration (or an amount of consideration is due) from the                   貨品或服務的責任。
              customer.

     Existence of significant financing component                                  存在重大融資成分

     the associated goods or services is less than one year, the Group             於一年的合約而言，本集團就任何重大
     applies the practical expedient of not adjusting the transaction price        融資成分應用不調整交易價格的實際權
     for any significant financing component.                                      宜方法。

     Revenue from the sales of products (mainly including coke and                 直接向客戶銷售產品（主要包括焦炭及
     chemicals products) directly to customers is recognized when                  化工產品）的收益於貨品控制權已轉讓

     products are accepted by the customers at the customer’s specific             點或本集團廠房接收時。有關客戶就產
     destination or the Group’s plants. The customers have full discretion         品全權控制，並概無可影響客戶接受產
     over the products, and there is no unfulfilled obligation that could          品而尚未達成的責任。本集團於貨品交
     affect the customers’ acceptance of the products. A receivable                付至客戶時確認應收款項，原因是收取
     is recognized by the Group when the goods are delivered to the                代價的權利於這時間點變為無條件，僅
     customers as this represents the point in time at which the right to          須待時過去便可收取付款。所購買的產

     required before payment is due. Customers have no right to return

     Principal versus agent                                                        委託人與代理人

     When another party is involved in providing goods or services to a            當另一方涉及向客戶提供商品或服務
     customer, the Group determines whether the nature of its promise is           時，本集團釐定其承諾之性質是否為提
     a performance obligation to provide the specified goods or services           供指定貨品或服務本身之履約責任（即
     itself (i.e. the Group is a principal) or to arrange for those goods          本集團為委託人）或安排由另一方提供
     or services to be provided by the other party (i.e. the Group is an           該等貨品或服務（即本集團為代理人）。
     agent).

     The Group is a principal if it controls the specified good or service         倘本集團在向客戶轉讓貨品或服務之前
     before that good or service is transferred to a customer.                     控制指定貨品或服務，則本集團為委託
                                                                                   人。

     The Group is an agent if its performance obligation is to arrange for         倘本集團之履約責任為安排另一方提供
     the provision of the specified good or service by another party. In           指定的貨品或服務，則本集團為代理
     this case, the Group does not control the specified good or service           人。在此情況下，在將貨品或服務轉讓
     provided by another party before that good or service is transferred          予客戶之前，本集團不控制另一方提供
     to the customer. When the Group acts as an agent, it recognizes               的指定貨品或服務。當本集團為代理人
     revenue in the amount of any fee or commission to which it expects            時，應就為換取另一方安排提供的指定
     to be entitled in exchange for arranging for the specified goods or           貨品或服務預期有權取得之任何費用或
     services to be provided by the other party.                                   佣金之金額確認收益。

              Contract costs                                                                合同成本

              Costs to fulfil a contract                                                    履行合同的成本

              The Group incurs costs to fulfil a contract in its sales of products          本集團於產品銷售及服務合約中產生履
              and service contracts. The Group first assesses whether these                 行合同的成本。本集團首先根據其他相
              costs qualify for recognition as an asset in terms of other relevant          關準則評估該等成本是否合資格確認為
              standards, failing which it recognizes an asset for these costs only          資產，倘不合資格，僅在符合以下標準
              if they meet all of the following criteria:                                   後將該等成本確認為資產：

              •       the costs relate directly to a contract or to an anticipated          •    有關成本與本集團可明確識別的
                      contract that the Group can specifically identify;                         合同或預期合同有直接關係；

              •       the costs generate or enhance resources of the Group                  •   有關成本令本集團將用於履行
                      that will be used in satisfying (or in continuing to satisfy)             （或持續履行）日後履約義務之資
                      performance obligations in the future; and                                源得以產生或有所增加；及

              •       the costs are expected to be recovered.                               •    有關成本預期可收回。

              The asset so recognized is subsequently amortized to profit or                由此確認的資產隨後按系統性基準攤銷
              loss on a systematic basis that is consistent with the transfer to the        並計入損益，與轉讓予客戶商品或服務
              customer of the goods or services to which the assets relate. The             （與資產相關）相符。資產須進行減值審
              asset is subject to impairment review.                                        閱。

              Lease                                                                         租賃

              Definition of a lease                                                         租賃的定義

              A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right         倘合約給予權利在一段時間內使用已識
              to control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in             別資產以換取代價，則該合約為或包含
              exchange for consideration.                                                   租賃。

              For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of initial        就於首次應用國際財務報告準則第16
              application of IFRS 16 or arising from business combinations, the             號當日或之後簽訂或修訂的或因業務合
              Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease based on             併而產生的合約而言，本集團於開始或
              the definition under IFRS 16 at inception or modification date. Such          修訂日期根據於國際財務報告準則第
              contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and conditions of            16號項下的定義評估合約是否為或包
              the contract are subsequently changed.                                        含租賃。該合約將不會被重新評估，除
                                                                                            非該合約中的條款與條件隨後被改動。

     The Group as a lessee                                                         本集團作為承租人

     Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                       分配至合約組成部分的代價

     For a contract that contains a lease component and one or more                就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或多項額
     additional lease or non-lease components, the Group allocates the             外租賃或非租賃組成部分的合約而言，
     consideration in the contract to each lease component on the basis            本集團根據租賃組成部分的相關獨立價
     of the relative stand-alone price of the lease component and the              格及非租賃組成部分的獨立價格總額於
     aggregate stand-alone price of the non-lease components.                      合約分配代價至各租賃組成部分。

     The Group applies practical expedient not to separate non-lease               本集團亦採用實際權宜方法，不將非租
     components from lease component, and instead account for the                  賃組成部分與租賃組成部分分開，反而
     lease component and any associated non-lease components as a                  將租賃組成部分及任何相關非租賃組成
     single lease component.                                                       部分入賬列為單一租賃組成部分。

     Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                              短期租賃及低價值資產租賃

     The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption                  本集團將短期租賃確認豁免應用於租
     to leases that have a lease term of 12 months or less from the                賃，該租賃的租期自開始日期起計為期
     commencement date and do not contain a purchase option. It also               十二個月或以下並且不包括購買選擇
     applies the recognition exemption for lease of low-value assets.              權。本集團亦對低價值資產租賃應用確
     Lease payments on short-term leases and leases of low-value assets            認豁免。短期租賃及低價值資產租賃的
     are recognized as expense on a straight-line basis over the lease             租賃付款按直線基準於租期內確認為開
     term.                                                                         支。

     Right-of-use assets                                                           使用權資產

     The cost of right-of-use assets includes:                                     使用權資產的成本包括：

     •     the amount of the initial measurement of the lease liability;           •     租賃負債的初步計量金額；

     •     any lease payments made at or before the commencement                   •     於開始日期或之前所作的任何租
           date, less any lease incentives received; and                                 賃付款，減去所得的任何租賃優
                                                                                         惠；及

     •     any initial direct costs incurred by the Group.                         •     本集團發生的任何初始直接成
                                                                                         本。

     Right-of-use assets are measured at cost, less any accumulated                使用權資產按成本減任何累計折舊及減
     depreciation and impairment losses, and adjusted for any                      值虧損計量，並就租賃負債的任何重新
     remeasurement of lease liabilities.                                           計量作出調整。

              entity, transactions in currencies other than the entity’s functional         以該實體功能貨幣以外的貨幣（外幣）列
              currency (foreign currencies) are recognized at the rates of                  值的交易按交易日期的現行匯率確認。
              exchanges prevailing on the dates of the transactions. At the end             於各報告期末，以外幣列值的貨幣項目

              component of that gain or loss is also recognised in profit or loss.          或虧損於其他全面收益確認時，該收益
              When a fair value gain or loss on a non-monetary item is recognized           或虧損之任何匯兌部分亦於其他全面收
              in other comprehensive income, any exchange component of that                 益確認。以歷史成本計量的以外幣列值
              gain or loss is also recognised in other comprehensive income.                的非貨幣項目不予重新換算。
              Non-monetary items that are measured in terms of historical cost in

              and on the retranslation of monetary items are recognized in profit           生的匯兌差額於其產生期間在損益內確
              or loss in the period in which they arise.                                    認。

              Taxation                                                                       稅項

              Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently payable             所得稅開支指現時應付的稅項及遞延稅
              and deferred tax.                                                              項總額。

              The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.             現時應付的稅項乃以年內的應課稅溢利
              Taxable profit differs from profit before taxation because of income           為基準。應課稅溢利與除稅前溢利有
              or expense that are taxable or deductible in other years and                   別，這是由於其他年度應課稅或可扣除
              items that are never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for          的收入或開支項目以及永不課稅或不可

              substantively enacted by the end of each reporting period.                     的負債按各報告期末前已頒佈或大致上
                                                                                             已頒佈的稅率計算。

              Deferred tax is recognized on temporary differences between                    遞延稅項指就按照於綜合財務報表呈列
              the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated             資產及負債賬面值與根據用作計算應課
              financial statements and the corresponding tax base used in the                稅溢利的相關稅基間所確認的暫時差
              computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally          異。遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課稅暫
              recognized for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets          時差異確認。遞延稅項資產則一般在應
              are generally recognized for all deductible temporary differences              課稅溢利可能將用作抵銷可動用的應扣
              to the extent that is probable that taxable profits will be available          除暫時差異時確認所有應扣除暫時差
              against which those deductible temporary differences can be                    異。倘因初步確認交易中資產與負債
              utilized. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognized          （業務合併除外）而引起暫時差異或對
              if the temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other         應課稅溢利或會計溢利均無影響時，將
              than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in a                 不會確認該等遞延稅項資產與負債。此
              transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting         外，倘暫時差異產生自商譽初步確認，
              profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognized if the        則不會確認遞延稅項負債。
              temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of goodwill.

     ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)

     Taxation (Continued)                                                              稅項（續）

     Deferred tax liabilities are recognized for taxable temporary                     遞延稅項負債乃按與於附屬公司的投資
     difference associated with investments in subsidiaries and interests              及於聯營公司和合營企業的權益相關的
     in associates and joint ventures, except where the Group is able to               應課稅暫時差異而確認，惟本集團有能
     control the reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable               力控制暫時差異的轉撥及暫時差異將可
     that the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable                 能不會於可見將來轉撥除外，而因與該
     future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary                     等投資及權益有關的應扣除暫時差異而
     differences associated with such investments and interests are                    產生的遞延稅項資產，僅在可能有足夠
     only recognized to the extent that it is probable that there will be              應課稅溢利以動用暫時差異利益及預期
     sufficient taxable profits against which to utilize the benefits of the           於可見將來轉撥時確認。
     temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in the
     foreseeable future.

     The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the end of              遞延稅項資產賬面值乃於各報告期末審
     each reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is no longer              閱，並減至再無足夠應課稅溢利以收回
     probable that sufficient taxable profits will be available to allow all or        全部或部分資產。
     part of the asset to be recovered.

     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates that            遞延稅項資產及負債乃以預期於償付負
     are expected to apply in the period in which the liability is settled             債或變現資產的期間應用的稅率，根據
     or the asset is realized, based on tax rates (and tax laws) that have             於各報告期末已頒佈或實質上已頒佈的
     been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of each reporting                稅率（及稅法）計算。
     period.

     The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects the               遞延稅項負債及資產的計算結果反映本
     tax consequences that would follow from the manner in which the                   集團於各報告期末預期就收回或償付資
     Group expects, at the end of each reporting period, to recover or                 產及負債賬面值可能引致的稅務後果。
     settle the carrying amounts of its assets and liabilities.

     For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for leasing transactions               就計量本集團確認使用權資產及相關租
     in which the Group recognizes the right-of-use assets and the                     賃負債的租賃交易的遞延稅項而言，本
     related lease liabilities, the Group first determines whether the tax             集團首先釐定稅項扣減是否歸因於使用
     deductions are attributable to the right-of-use assets or the lease               權資產或租賃負債。
     liabilities.

     For leasing transactions in which the tax deductions are attributable             就租賃負債應佔稅項扣減的租賃交易而
     to the lease liabilities, the Group applies IAS 12 Income Taxes                   言，本集團對使用權資產及租賃負債分
     requirements to right-of-use assets and lease liabilities separately.                         「所得稅」
                                                                                       別應用國際會計準則第12號
     Temporary differences relating to right-of-use assets and lease                   之規定。因應用初始確認豁免，與使用
     liabilities are not recognized at initial recognition and over the lease          權資產及租賃負債有關之暫時性差額並
     terms due to application of the initial recognition exemption.                    未於初始確認時及於租賃期內確認。

              Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there is a legally           如有法定可執行權利將即期稅項資產抵
              enforceable right to set off current tax assets against current tax              銷即期稅項負債，而遞延稅項資產及負

              taxable entity by the same taxation authority.                                   的所得稅有關，則遞延稅項資產及負債
                                                                                               可相互抵銷。

              when it relates to items that are recognized in other comprehensive              其他全面收益或直接於權益確認的項目
              income or directly in equity, in which case, the current and deferred            相關者除外，於該情況下，即期及遞延
              tax are also recognized in other comprehensive income or directly                稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或直接於權
              in equity respectively. Where current tax or deferred tax arises from            益確認。倘因對業務合併進行初始會計
              the initial accounting for a business combination, the tax effect is             處理而產生即期稅項或遞延稅項，則稅
              included in the accounting for the business combination.                         務影響計入業務合併的會計處理內。

              In assessing any uncertainty over income tax treatments, the Group               於評估所得稅處理的任何不確定性時，

              the current and deferred taxes are determined consistently with the              如果接納，即期及遞延稅項會與填報所
              tax treatment in the income tax filings. If it is not probable that the          得稅時之稅務處理一致地釐定。如果有
              relevant taxation authority will accept an uncertain tax treatment, the          關稅務部門不可能接納不確定的稅務處
              effect of each uncertainty is reflected by using either the most likely          理，每個不確定性之影響利用最大可能
              amount or the expected value.                                                    金額或預期值反映。

     Inventories                                                                    存貨

     Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realizable value.          存貨乃按成本及可變現淨值兩者中較低
     Costs of inventories are determined using weighted average cost                者列賬。存貨成本按加權平均成本公式
     formula. Net realizable value represents the estimated selling price           計算。可變現淨值指存貨估計銷售價格
     for inventories less all estimated costs to completion and the costs           減去完成時的所有估計成本及進行銷售
     necessary to make the sale. Costs necessary to make the sale                   所需的成本。進行銷售所需的成本包括
     include incremental costs directly attributable to the sale and non-           直接歸屬於銷售的增量成本及本集團進
     incremental costs which the Group must incur to make the sale.                 行銷售須產生的非增量成本。

     Properties for/under development which are intended to be sold                 於發展完成後擬作出售之待發展╱發展
     upon completion of development and properties for sale are                     中物業及待售物業均分類為流動資產。
     classified as current assets. Except for the leasehold land element            除租賃土地部分按照使用權資產之會計
     which is measured at cost model in accordance with the accounting              政策以成本模式計量外，待發展╱發展
     policies of right-of-use assets, properties for/under development/             中物業╱待售物業均以成本及可變現淨
     properties for sale are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable        值兩者之較低者列賬。成本按特定識別
     value. Cost is determined on a specific identification basis including         基準釐定，包括分配所產生之有關開發
     allocation of the related development expenditure incurred and                 開支及（倘適用）資本化借貸成本。可變
     where appropriate, borrowing costs capitalised. Net realisable                 現淨值指物業估計售價減完成之估計成
     value represents the estimated selling price for the properties less           本及作出銷售之必要成本。進行銷售之
     estimated cost to completion and costs necessary to make the                   所需成本包括銷售直接應佔之增量成本
     sales. Costs necessary to make the sale include incremental costs              及本集團為進行銷售而必須涉及之非增
     directly attributable to the sale and non-incremental costs which the          量成本。
     Group must incur to make the sale.

              Provisions                                                                      撥備

              Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present obligation               倘本集團因過往事件須承擔現時責任
              (legal or constructive) as a result of a past event, it is probable that        （法定或推定），則本集團須履行該責
              the Group will be required to settle that obligation, and a reliable            任，且該責任所涉金額能夠可靠估計，
              estimate can be made of the amount of the obligation.                           則確認撥備。

              The amount recognised as a provision is the best estimate of the                經考慮與責任有關之風險及不確定因素
              consideration required to settle the present obligation at the end of           後，確認為撥備之金額為於報告期末清
              the reporting period, taking into account the risks and uncertainties           償現時責任所需代價之最佳估計。倘撥
              surrounding the obligation. When a provision is measured using the              備乃以估計用作清償現時責任之現金流
              cash flows estimated to settle the present obligation, its carrying             量計量，則其賬面值為該等現金流量之
              amount is the present value of those cash flows (where the effect of            現值（倘貨幣時值影響屬重大）。
              the time value of money is material).

              Contingent liabilities                                                          或然負債

              A contingent liability is a present obligation arising from past events         或然負債指因已發生的事件而產生的現
              but is not recognised because it is not probable that an outflow of             有責任，但由於可能不需要流出具有經
              resources embodying economic benefits will be required to settle                濟利益的資源以履行責任，或責任金額
              the obligation or the amount of the obligation cannot be measured               未能充分可靠地計量，故不予確認。
              with sufficient reliability.

              Where the Group is jointly and severally liable for an obligation, the          倘本集團對一項義務承擔連帶及個別責
              part of the obligation that is expected to be met by other parties              任，則該責任中預期由其他方履行的部
              is treated as a contingent liability and it is not recognised in the            分被視為或然負債，且不在綜合財務報
              consolidated financial statements.                                              表中確認。

              The Group assesses continually to determine whether an outflow of               本集團持續進行評估，以確定具有經濟
              resources embodying economic benefits has become probable. If                   利益的資源是否可能流出。倘先前作為
              it becomes probable that an outflow of future economic benefits will            或然負債處理的項目將可能需要未來經
              be required for an item previously dealt with as a contingent liability,        濟利益流出，則在發生概率變動的報告
              a provision is recognised in the consolidated financial statements              期內於綜合財務報表中確認撥備，惟極
              in the reporting period in which the change in probability occurs,              少數情況下無法進行可靠估計的情況除
              except in the extremely rare circumstances where no reliable                    外。
              estimate can be made.

     Financial instruments                                                            金融工具

     Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognized when a                 當集團實體成為工具合約條文之訂約
     group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the                方，則會確認金融資產及金融負債。所
     instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of financial assets               有常規金融資產買賣均按交易日期基準
     are recognized and derecognized on a trade day basis. Regular                    確認及終止確認。常規買賣為須按市場
     way purchases or sales are purchases or sales of financial assets                規管或慣例確立之時限內交付資產的金
     that require delivery of assets within the time frame established by             融資產購買或出售。
     regulation or convention in the market place.

     Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured at             金融資產及金融負債初步按公允值計
     fair value except for trade receivables arising from contracts with              量，惟客戶合約產生的貿易應收款項
     customers which are initially measured in accordance with IFRS                   初步根據國際財務報告準則第15號計
     15. Transaction costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition          量。收購或發行金融資產及金融負債
     or issue of financial assets and financial liabilities (other than               （除按公允值計入損益的金融資產及金
     financial assets and financial liabilities at FVTPL) are added to or             融負債以外）直接應佔的交易成本於初
     deducted from the fair value of the financial assets or financial                步確認時計入金融資產或金融負債（按
     liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition. Transaction costs           適用情況）的公允值或於其中扣除。收
     directly attributable to the acquisition of financial assets or financial        購按公允值計入損益的金融資產或金融
     liabilities at FVTPL are recognized immediately in profit or loss.               負債直接應佔的交易成本於損益即時確
                                                                                      認。

     The effective interest method is a method of calculating the                     實際利率法是一種計算相關報告期內金
     amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of                融資產或金融負債的攤銷成本以及分配
     allocating interest income and interest expense over the relevant                利息收入和利息開支的方法。實際利率
     period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts           為於金融資產或金融負債的預期存續期
     estimated future cash receipts and payments (including all fees and              或（如適用）更短期間內能夠精確計算預
     points paid or received that form an integral part of the effective              計未來現金收入及款項（包括構成實際
     interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or discounts)                利率不可或缺部分之一切已付或已收費
     through the expected life of the financial asset or financial liability,         用及點數、交易成本及其他溢價或折
     or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount              讓）貼現至初步確認時賬面淨值的利率。
     on initial recognition.

     Amortized cost and interest income                                             攤銷成本及利息收入

     Interest income is recognized using the effective interest method              其後按攤銷成本計量的金融資產及其後
     for financial assets measured subsequently at amortized cost and               按公允值計入其他全面收益的應收款項
     receivables subsequently measured at FVTOCI. Interest income                   使用實際利率法確認利息收入。利息收
     is calculated by applying the effective interest rate to the gross             入透過將實際利率應用於金融資產的總
     carrying amount of a financial asset, except for financial assets              賬面值計算，惟其後已出現信貸減值的
     that have subsequently become credit-impaired (see below). For                 金融資產除外（見下文）。就其後已出現
     financial assets that have subsequently become credit-impaired,                信貸減值的金融資產而言，利息收入乃
     interest income is recognized by applying the effective interest rate          透過將實際利率應用於下一個報告期的
     to the amortized cost of the financial asset from the next reporting           金融資產的攤銷成本進行確認。倘出現
     period. If the credit risk on the credit-impaired financial instrument         信貸減值的金融工具的信貸風險有所改
     improves so that the financial asset is no longer credit-impaired,             善，以致於該金融資產不再出現信貸減
     interest income is recognized by applying the effective interest               值，則利息收入乃透過將實際利率應用
     rate to the gross carrying amount of the financial asset from the              於釐定該資產不再出現信貸減值後的報
     beginning of the reporting period following the determination that             告期初之金融資產的總賬面值進行確
     the asset is no longer credit-impaired.                                        認。

     default events over the expected life of the relevant instrument.              期內發生的所有可能違約事件所導致的
     In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the portion of                預期信貸虧損。與此相對，12個月預
     lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events that are           期信貸虧損（「12個月預期信貸虧損」）
     possible within 12 months after the end of each reporting period.              指預期於各報告期末後12個月內可能
     Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss               發生的違約事件所導致的部分全期預期
     experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,             信貸虧損。評估根據本集團過往信貸虧

                                                                                    調整。

     a provision matrix with appropriate groupings except that significant          損使用具有適當分組的撥備矩陣進行集
     balances are assessed individually.                                            體評估（重大結餘個別評估除外）。

     period with the risk if a default occurring on the financial instrument        初始確認日期出現違約的風險。作此評
     as at the date of initial recognition. In making this assessment, the          估時，本集團會考慮合理及有理據的定
     Group considers both quantitative and qualitative information that             量及定性資料，包括歷史經驗及毋須花
     is reasonable and supportable, including historical experience and             費不必要成本或精力即可獲得的前瞻性
     forward-looking information that is available without undue cost or            資料。
     effort.

     Definition of default                                                               違約的定義

     For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an event                   就內部信貸風險管理而言，當內部產生

              have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows of                流造成負面影響的違約事件發生時，即

              is credit-impaired includes observable data about the following                產出現信貸減值的證據包括涉及以下事
              events:                                                                        件的可觀察數據：

              •       significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower;        •   發行方或借款人遇到嚴重財政困
                                                                                                 難；

                                                                                                 況；

                      reasons relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty, having            合約考慮，給予借款人在其他情

                      would not otherwise consider;

              •       it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter                   •   借款人有可能破產或進行其他財
                      bankruptcy or other financial reorganization.                              務重組。

              Write-off policy                                                               撇銷政策

              The Group writes off a financial asset when there is information               當有資料顯示對手方陷入嚴重財務困

              there is no realistic prospect of recovery, e.g. when the counterparty         對手方已清算或進入破產程序），本集
              has been placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy               團會撇銷金融資產。已撇銷的金融資產
              proceedings. Financial assets written off may still be subject to              仍可根據本集團的收回程序實施強制執
              enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery procedures,                  行，在適當情況下考慮法律意見。任何
              taking into account legal advice where appropriate. Any recoveries             收回款項會於損益中確認。
              made are recognized in profit or loss.

     Measurement and recognition of ECL                                               預期信貸虧損的計量及確認

     The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of default,              預期信貸虧損的計量乃指違約概率、違
     loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if there is a default)        約損失率（即倘違約引致損失的程度）及
     and the exposure at default. The assessment of the probability                   達約風險的函數。評估違約概率及違約
     of default and loss given default is based on historical data and                損失率根據過往數據及前瞻性資料而作
     forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased              出。預期信貸虧損的估計反映無偏頗及
     and probability-weighted amount that is determined with the                      概率加權的數額，其乃根據加權的相應
     respective risks of default occurring as the weights. The Group                  違約風險蘆定。本集團採用可行權宜方
     uses a practical expedient in estimating ECL on trade receivables                法，利用撥備矩陣估計貿易應收款項的
     using a provision matrix taking into consideration historical credit             預期信貸虧捐，當中計及過往信貸虧損
     loss experience, adjusted for forward looking information that is                經驗，並根據毋須付出不必要努力即可
     available without undue cost and effort.                                         獲得的前職性資料進行調整。

     Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference between all                    一般而言，預期信貸虧損估計為本集團
     contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance                   根據合約應收的所有合約現金流量與本
     with the contract and all the cash flows that the Group expects to               集團預計收取的所有現金流量之間的差
     receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined at initial         額，並按初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼
     recognition.                                                                     現。

     For a financial guarantee contract, the Group is required to make                就財務擔保合約而言，本集團須僅在債
     payments only in the event of a default by the debtor in accordance              務人違反保證文書條款的情況下作出付
     with the terms of the instrument that is guaranteed. Accordingly, the            款。因此，預期信貸虧損是償還持有人
     ECL is the present value of the expected payments to reimburse                   信貸損失的預期付款的現值，該信貸損
     the holder for a credit loss that it incurs less any amounts that the            失減去本集團預期從持有人、債務人或
     Group expects to receive from the holder, the debtors or any other               任何其他方獲得的任何金額。
     party.

     For ECL on financial guarantee contracts for which the effective                 就無法釐定實際利率的財務擔保合約的
     interest rate cannot be determined, the Group will apply a discount              預期信貸虧損而言，本集團將採納反映
     rate that reflects the current market assessment of the time value of            目前對貨幣時間價值的市場評估及該現
     money and the risks that are specific to the cash flows but only if,             金流特定風險的貼現率，惟僅限於風險
     and to the extent that, the risks are taken into account by adjusting            按調整貼現率而非調整所貼現的現金不
     the discount rate instead of adjusting the cash shortfalls being                 足納入考慮時。
     discounted.

     the proceeds received.                                                             押借貸。

35. RESERVES                                                                 35. 儲備

   Share premium                                                                 股份溢價

   Under the Companies Law (Revised) of the Cayman Islands,                      根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），計入本
   amount included in the share premium account of the Company                   公司股份溢價賬中的金額可分派予本公
   is distributable to the shareholders of the Company provided that             司股東，前提是緊隨建議分派股息日期
   immediately following the date on which the dividend is proposed to           後本公司將有能力在日常業務過程中償
   be distributed, the Company will be in a position to pay off its debts        還到期債務。
   as and when they fall due in the ordinary course of business.

   Merger reserve                                                                合併儲備

   The merger reserve represents the difference between the nominal              合併儲備指本公司已發行股本面值╱已
   value of the share capital issued/consideration paid by the                   付代價與根據本集團重組所收購附屬公
   Company and the aggregate capital of the subsidiaries acquired                司合計資本之間的差額，根據合併會計
   pursuant to the group reorganization, accounted for as capital                基準作為最終控股公司的注資入賬╱分
   contribution from/distribution to ultimate holding company under              派。
   merger basis of accounting.

   During the year ended December 31, 2021, the Group acquired                   截至2021年12月31日止年度期間，本
   100% equity interest or share capital of RMB205 million in Tangshan           集團以現金收購唐山旭陽化工有限公司
   Risun Petroleum at the consideration of RMB570 million paid in                100%權益或股本人民幣205,000,000
   cash and accounted for as business acquisition under common                   元，代價為人民幣570,000,000元，作
   control.                                                                      為共同控制下的業務收購入賬。

   Reserve fund                                                                  儲備基金

   The Articles of Association of certain PRC subsidiaries require the           若干中國附屬公司的組織章程細則要求
   appropriation of 10% of their profit after tax each year, based on            每年根據其法定經審核財務報表將其除
   their statutory audited financial statements, to the reserve fund until       稅後溢利的10%撥入儲備基金，直至
   the balance reaches 50% of the registered capital of the respective           結餘達到相應中國附屬公司註冊資本的
   PRC subsidiaries. The reserve fund may be capitalized as the paid-            50%。儲備基金可資本化為該等附屬公
   in capital of these subsidiaries.                                             司的實收資本。

   Safety fund                                                                   安全基金

   Pursuant to relevant PRC regulation, the Group is required to                 根據相關中國法規，本集團須將核心業
   transfer 1.5% on revenue generated from the core business into a              務產生的收益1.5%轉入指定基金。該
   designated fund. The fund will be used for installation and repair            基金將用於安全設施的安裝、維修和保
   and maintenance of safety facilities. The movements in the reporting          養。於報告期內變動指根據中國相關法
   periods represent the difference between the amounts provided                 規計提的金額與年內使用金額之間的差
   based on the relevant PRC regulation and the amount utilized                  額。
   during the year.

37. EMPLOYEE RETIREMENT BENEFITS                                          37. 僱員退休福利

   Defined contribution retirement plans                                       界定供款退休計劃

   Pursuant to the relevant labor rules and regulations in the PRC, the        根據中國相關勞工規則及法規，本集團
   PRC subsidiaries of the Group participate in defined contribution           中國附屬公司參與由中國市政府機關經
   retirement benefit schemes (the “Schemes”) organized by the PRC             營的界定供款退休福利計劃（「該等計
   municipal government authorities whereby the Group is required              劃」），據此，本集團須按適用比率向該
   to make contributions to the Schemes based on applicable rates.             等計劃作出供款。市政府機關負責全部
   The municipal government authorities are responsible for the entire         應付已退休僱員的退休金責任。
   pension obligations payable to retired employees.

   The Group also operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the              本集團亦根據香港強制性公積金計劃條
   “MPF scheme”) under the Hong Kong Mandatory Provident Fund                  例為於香港僱傭條例的司法管轄權下受
   Schemes Ordinance for employees employed under the jurisdiction             聘用且過往並非受界定福利退休計劃涵
   of the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance and not previously                    蓋的僱員，經營一項強制性公積金計劃
   covered by the defined benefit retirement plan. The MPF scheme is          （「強積金計劃」）。強積金計劃乃由獨立
   a defined contribution retirement plan administered by independent          受託人管理的界定供款退休計劃。根據
   trustees. Under the MPF scheme, the employer and its employees              強積金計劃，僱主及其僱員各自須按僱
   are each required to make contributions to the plan at 5% of the            員相關收入的5%向計劃作出供款，每
   employees’ relevant income, subject to a cap of monthly relevant            月相關收入上限為1,500港元。計劃供
   income of HK$1,500. Contributions to the plan vest immediately.             款即時歸屬。

39. CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                      39. 資本管理

    The Group’s primary objectives of managing capital are to                    本集團管理資本的主要目的是維護其持
    safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, and to         續經營的能力，從而使其能夠透過按合
    provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,        理成本取得融資，繼續為股東提供回報
    by securing access to finance at a reasonable cost.                          及為其他持份者提供利益。

    The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital             本集團積極及定期檢討和管理其資本架
    structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder               構，以在維持較高借款水平可能帶來的
    returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings, and         高股東回報取得平衡，並因應不同的經
    makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in            濟狀況為資本架構作出調整。
    economic conditions.

    Based on recommendations of the board of directors, the Group will           根據董事會建議，本集團將透過調整向
    balance its overall capital structure through adjusting the amount           股東派付的股息金額、發行新股、籌集
    of dividends paid to shareholders, issuing of new shares, raising            新債務融資或出售資產以減少債務，藉
    new debt financing or selling assets to reduce debt. During the              以平衡其整體資本架構。於報告期內，
    reporting period, there were no changes in the objectives, policies          並無更改目標、政策或程序。
    or processes.

   regularly review the market interest rates to capture the potential           場利率以把握減少借款成本的潛在機

   Group will enter into interest rate swap arrangement to mitigate the          率掉期安排以減低利率風險。

              A fundamental reform of major interest rate benchmarks has                全球正在對主要利率基準進行根本性改
              been undertaken globally to replace some interbank offered rates          革，將若干銀行同業拆息率替換為其他
              (“IBORs”) with alternative nearly risk-free rates. Details of the         近乎無風險的利率。利率基準改革對本
              impacts on the Group’s risk management strategy arising from              集團風險管理戰略之影響及實施可替換
              the interest rate benchmark reform and the progress towards               基準利率之進展載於本附註「利率基準
              implementation of alternative benchmark interest rates are set out        改革」。
              under “interest rate benchmark reform” in this note.

              Interest rate benchmark reform                                            利率基準改革

              As listed in note 31, the Group’s LIBOR bank loan will be subject to      誠如附註31所列，本集團的倫敦銀行
              the interest rate benchmark reform. The Group is closely monitoring       同業拆借利率銀行貸款將受到利率基準
              the market and managing the transition to new benchmark interest          改革影響。本集團正密切監測市場，並
              rates, including announcements made by the relevant IBOR                  管理新基準利率的過渡，包括相關倫敦
              regulators.                                                               銀行同業拆借利率監管機構作出的公
                                                                                        告。

              As at December 31, 2022, all LIBOR settings have been either              於2022年12月31日，所有倫敦銀行同
              ceased to be provided by any administrator or no longer be                業拆借利率設定將不再由任何管理者
              representative, except for US dollar settings (other than the 1-week      提供，或不再具代表性，惟將於緊接
              and 2-month settings) which will be ceased immediately after June         2023年6月30日後結束的美元設定除
              30, 2023.                                                                 外。

   There are fundamental differences between IBORs and the various                倫敦銀行同業拆借利率與各類可替代基
   alternative benchmark rates. IBORs are forward looking term rates              準利率間存在重大差異。倫敦銀行同業
   published for a period (e.g. 3 months) at the beginning of that                拆借利率為於某期初就一段期間（例如
   period and include an inter-bank credit spread, whereas alternative            3個月）發佈的前瞻性期限利率，並包
   benchmark rates are typically risk-free overnight rates published at           括銀行間信貸息差，而可替代基準利率
   the end of the overnight period with no embedded credit spread.                通常為於隔夜期末發佈無嵌入信貸息差
   These differences will result in additional uncertainty regarding              的無風險隔夜利率。該等差異將導致有
   floating rate interest payments.                                               關浮息利率付款的額外不確定性。

   Liquidity risk                                                                 流動資金風險

   The additional uncertainty on various alternative rates which are              各類可替代利率的額外不確定性通常按
   typically published on overnight basis will require additional liquidity       隔夜基礎發佈，將需要額外流動資金管
   management. The Group has established liquidity risk management                理。本集團已制訂流動資金風險管理政
   policy to ensure sufficient liquid resources to accommodate                    策，以確保擁有充足流動資源，應對隔
   unexpected increases in overnight rates.                                       夜利率不可預期的增長。

   Litigation risk                                                                訴訟風險

   If no agreement is reached to implement the interest rate                      各類可替代利率的額外不確定性通常按
   benchmark reform on contracts which have not been transitioned                 隔夜基礎發佈，將需要額外流動資金管
   to the relevant alternative benchmark rates (e.g. arising from                 理。本集團已制訂流動資金風險管理政
   differing interpretation of existing fallback terms), there is a risk of       策，以確保擁有充足流動資源，應對隔
   prolonged disputes with counterparties which could give rise to                夜利率不可預期的增長。
   additional legal and other costs. The Group is working closely with
   all counterparties to avoid this from occurring.

   alternative benchmark rate and the Directors is of the view that the           銀行協商釐定替代基準利率，董事認
